/openapi.json

default

Earlier this year, Google launched AI Mode https://search.google/ways-to-search/ai-mode/, which answers questions in a conversational way and more features to give you everything you need during the planning process. There’s so much to know, so sit back and save this for later! \r\n\r\n**✈️ Find flights through a chat box**\r\n\r\nI love that Gemini lets you just type what you want and spits out flight options. We no longer have to toggle with filters. It’s super convenient, and I appreciate Google’s real-time data.\r\n\r\n**&#127796; Let Google Flight Deals show you the world**\r\n\r\nFlight Deals aren't just about cheap tickets. You can ask for vague getaway ideas and it throws options at you you’d never have thought of. I got Belize trip options way cheaper than usual just by asking for a birthday weekend on the Caribbean sea. \r\n\r\n**&#128142; Create a profile with Gems**\r\n\r\nGems are like giving Gemini a cheat sheet on your travel style. I have one for group trips, family trips, and business travel. Once you set it up, Gemini remembers your preferences, so you don’t have to repeat yourself every time. It saves me a ton of brain space.\r\n\r\n**&#128194; Giving is receiving**\r\n\r\nFeed Gemini your emails, photos, and docs and it’ll tailor trips like a personal travel assistant. Feels a little weird, but the results are there! It pulled info from a past conference and suggested flights, hotels, and even a restaurant that matched my past choices.\r\n\r\n**&#128373;️‍♀️ Use AI to fact-check AI**\r\n\r\nEven the smartest AI can mess up sometimes and not be fully accurate. I had Gemini list restaurants, hotels, and flights, then double-checked everything. Most of it was solid, but a couple of details were technically right but a little off for the vibe I wanted. AI is great, but human eyes and research still matter.\r\n\r\nHopefully the next time you open up the search engine to browse a trip, you remember these tricks to make it all easier on you.\r\n\r\n \r\n\r\n**| CRUISES**\r\n\r\nhttps://www.cntraveler.com/story/new-africa-cruise-itineraries-are-coming-to-both-sides-of-the-continent \r\n\r\n**Explore Africa by Sea on New Itineraries**\r\n\r\nAfrica hasn’t exactly been a hotspot for cruises, until now. Earlier this year, Google launched AI Mode https://search.google/ways-to-search/ai-mode/, which answers questions in a conversational way and more features to give you everything you need during the planning process. There’s so much to know, so sit back and save this for later! \r\n\r\n**✈️ Find flights through a chat box**\r\n\r\nI love that Gemini lets you just type what you want and spits out flight options. We no longer have to toggle with filters. It’s super convenient, and I appreciate Google’s real-time data.\r\n\r\n**&#127796; Let Google Flight Deals show you the world**\r\n\r\nFlight Deals aren't just about cheap tickets. You can ask for vague getaway ideas and it throws options at you you’d never have thought of. I got Belize trip options way cheaper than usual just by asking for a birthday weekend on the Caribbean sea. \r\n\r\n**&#128142; Create a profile with Gems**\r\n\r\nGems are like giving Gemini a cheat sheet on your travel style. I have one for group trips, family trips, and business travel. Once you set it up, Gemini remembers your preferences, so you don’t have to repeat yourself every time. It saves me a ton of brain space.\r\n\r\n**&#128194; Giving is receiving**\r\n\r\nFeed Gemini your emails, photos, and docs and it’ll tailor trips like a personal travel assistant. Feels a little weird, but the results are there! It pulled info from a past conference and suggested flights, hotels, and even a restaurant that matched my past choices.\r\n\r\n**&#128373;️‍♀️ Use AI to fact-check AI**\r\n\r\nEven the smartest AI can mess up sometimes and not be fully accurate. I had Gemini list restaurants, hotels, and flights, then double-checked everything. Most of it was solid, but a couple of details were technically right but a little off for the vibe I wanted. AI is great, but human eyes and research still matter.\r\n\r\nHopefully the next time you open up the search engine to browse a trip, you remember these tricks to make it all easier on you.\r\n\r\n \r\n\r\n**| CRUISES**\r\n\r\nhttps://www.cntraveler.com/story/new-africa-cruise-itineraries-are-coming-to-both-sides-of-the-continent \r\n\r\n**Explore Africa by Sea on New Itineraries**\r\n\r\nAfrica hasn’t exactly been a hotspot for cruises, until now. New itineraries https://www.cntraveler.com/story/new-africa-cruise-itineraries-are-coming-to-both-sides-of-the-continent are popping up on both sides of the continent, giving travelers a chance to see more without the usual crowds that you bump into on land. \r\n\r\n**Itineraries around the continent: **\r\n\r\n&#127480;&#127464; Seychelles & Outer Islands - Smaller ships like Ponant’s Le Bougainville https://us.ponant.com/cruises/on-board/vessel-le-bougainville can access remote spots with minimal impact. You can earn two elite qualifying nights per eligible stay at any Hyatt hotel or resort. With a status like that, you get two nights for every $5,000 spent on the card plus all the perks of the lower tier, like waived resort fees, bonus points, elite reservation lines, and more. If you spend even more, you can qualify for Hyatt’s top Globalist tier as well.\r\n\r\n&#128184;\r\n\r\n**Our take**\r\n\r\nA deal like this doesn’t come by very often, and given how long it can take to earn any type of hotel status, this is definitely a good way to surpass that obstacle.\r\n\r\nThis bonus is awesome, but I would also just generally recommend the Hyatt card to anyone. Hyatt’s reward program is pretty solid, and the more you spend, the more you earn, and it’s a lot easier to rack up award nights on this card than most. So, get this card for the bonus, but stay for the long-term benefits. Yesterday, the European Union (EU)officially launched https://www.euronews.com/travel/2025/10/10/eu-announces-launch-date-for-entryexit-system-heres-how-travellers-can-prepare the long-awaited (and many times delayed) Entry/Exit System, or EES, for all non-EU nationals. \r\n\r\nThe EES will slowly roll out over the next six months, but the first countries that participated in the rollout included Estonia, Luxembourg, the Czech Republic, and select Swiss and German airports. Spain also tested a single flight yesterday at Madrid airport. \r\n\r\n✅\r\n\r\n**What do travelers need to know?**\r\n\r\nEssentially, the new system will require travelers from the US, UK, and other non-EU countries to scan their passports and travel documents at a self-service kiosk. It does not apply to EU citizens or those with long-stay visas. \r\n\r\n⏱️\r\n\r\n**Should you expect delays?**\r\n\r\nAs with any new airport system, I would say it’d be safe to expect a few delays. Some experts even suggested arriving as much as three or four hours early to get through the checks. Longer wait times are expected as kinks are being worked out, so definitely use your discretion, especially if you’re traveling through any of the countries listed above. \r\n\r\n✈️\r\n\r\n**What else is changing?**\r\n\r\nFollowing the EES rollout, the European Travel Information and Authorization System (ETIAS) is expected to launch in late 2026. This will require non-EU visitors to complete a visa authorization before visiting via an online application, answer security questions, and pay a 20 Euro fee. It will be valid for three years or until the passport expiration date.\r\n\r\n**| MONEY MOVES**\r\n\r\nhttps://thedailynavigator.com/credit-cards/ways-to-use-chase-ink-unlimited-welcome-bonus/ \r\n\r\n**Limited-Time Offer Alert: Get $900 Cash Back**\r\n\r\nThis business credit card https://thedailynavigator.com/credit-cards/ways-to-use-chase-ink-unlimited-welcome-bonus/ is giving new applicants a massive welcome offer: $900 in cash back.\r\n\r\nBut here’s where it gets even better: thanks to a unique feature, that $900 can be converted into 90,000 points, worth $1,500+ in travel when transferred to partners like United, Marriott, Air Canada, and more.\r\n\r\n**With this bonus, you could:**\r\n\r\n* Pocket $900 in cash\r\n\r\n* Fly in one of the world’s best business class seats\r\n\r\n* Book a weekend at a luxury hotel\r\n\r\n* Snag a round-trip business class to Europe\r\n\r\n* Unlock United’s Excursionist Perk and get 3 flights for the price of 2\r\n\r\n* Cover multiple economy round-trips across the US\r\n\r\n* Book 3 round-trip flights to Europe in economy\r\n\r\nBig cash bonus = Even bigger travel potential.\r\n\r\n**Learn how it works here.** https://thedailynavigator.com/credit-cards/ways-to-use-chase-ink-unlimited-welcome-bonus/\r\n\r\n**| AROUND THE WORLD**\r\n\r\nFrom Iceland to the Philippines, here's what's happening around the world:\r\n\r\n&#127470;&#127480; This is the only US state https://www.travelandleisure.com/traveler-finds-diamond-at-crater-of-diamonds-state-park-arkansas-11790754 where you can score your own diamonds, so if you’re down to swing a shovel for your next jewelry piece, it’s worth the effort.\r\n\r\n**In today’s edition:**\r\n\r\n* Free flights are taking tourists across Thailand this fall \r\n\r\n* Southwest Rapid Rewards could soon take you to Asia\r\n\r\n* If you’re a “never-cruiser,” these small luxurious cruises are for you \r\n\r\nI dig it,\r\n\r\nKaylen\r\n\r\n \r\n\r\n**| TRAVEL**\r\n\r\nhttps://www.euronews.com/travel/2025/08/22/thailand-is-planning-to-give-away-thousands-of-free-domestic-flights-to-tourists \r\n\r\n**Tourists Get Thousands of Free Flights in Thailand**\r\n\r\n \r\n\r\nThailand always sounds like a good idea, especially this fall, as the country millions love is giving away free domestic flights for tourists who want to see more on their trips. Thailand plans to give 200,000 international visitors free domestic flights over the next three months, so Christmas has basically come early.\r\n\r\nThe **Buy International, Free Thailand Domestic Flights** https://www.euronews.com/travel/2025/08/22/thailand-is-planning-to-give-away-thousands-of-free-domestic-flights-to-tourists campaign lets eligible tourists claim a free round-trip within the country when booking international flights through participating airlines or travel agencies. Supported by the tourism ministry, the offer is set for September through November, pending cabinet approval, with the government covering up to THB 1,750 ($53.55) one-way or THB 3,500 ($107.10) round-trip, plus checked baggage (44 lbs) per ticket.\r\n\r\nThailand is giving away free domestic flights to push tourists beyond Bangkok, Phuket, and Chiang Mai, encouraging them to explore lesser-known places like Pai, Chiang Rai, and the islands of Trat, temples, local food scenes, and beaches. Six airlines (Thai Airways, Thai AirAsia, Bangkok Airways, Nok Air, Thai Lion Air, and Thai Vietjet) are on board, and officials hope the program will generate significant revenue, though only arrivals by air qualify, and pre-existing bookings don’t count.\r\n\r\nThailand is trying to bounce back after a rough few years, with tourist numbers down and safety concerns affecting its image. The government is offering easier visas and digital nomad options, with free domestic flights as the flashiest move.\r\n\r\n \r\n\r\n**| JOIN 1440**\r\n\r\nhttps://www.meintercept.com/3T3CM8/WGR393/?uid=15582&sub1=the_navigator&sub2=newsletter&sub3=08_25_2025 \r\n\r\n**Impartial News with 1440**\r\n\r\nTrusted by millions, 1440 https://www.meintercept.com/3T3CM8/WGR393/?uid=15582&sub1=the_navigator&sub2=newsletter&sub3=08_25_2025 provides daily fact-based insights across global events, business, politics, and culture.\r\n\r\nExperience clear knowledge in just five engaging minutes each day.\r\n\r\nSubscribe for free. https://www.meintercept.com/3T3CM8/WGR393/?uid=15582&sub1=the_navigator&sub2=newsletter&sub3=08_25_2025\r\n\r\n**| AIRLINES**\r\n\r\nhttps://www.southwest.com/customer-enhancements/airline-partnerships/#china-airlines \r\n\r\n**Your Southwest Points Could Stretch to Asia**\r\n\r\nI flew Southwest for the first time a few days ago, and I’ve got to say, the seats are comfy enough that I could handle a long haul in them. Now the airline’steaming up with Taiwan’s China Airlines https://www.southwest.com/customer-enhancements/airline-partnerships/#china-airlines, making West Coast connections much easier, with the whole trip bookable directly through China Airlines.\r\n\r\nBack in June 2025, Southwest announced the partnership with Taiwan’s China Airlines, and as of last week, the partnership is live! Travelers can finally book seamless itineraries combining both airlines. The new routes take off January 19, 2026, with five major West Coast airports to serve as gateways: Los Angeles (LAX), Phoenix (PHX), Seattle (SEA), San Francisco (SFO), and Ontario, California (ONT).\r\n\r\n**✈️**\r\n\r\n**How can I use my points? **\r\n\r\nThe partnership will start small, but it’s big news for Southwest Rapid Rewards members to soon earn and redeem points on flights to Asia. Southwest is still working on the tech to make booking and award redemptions available directly through its site and app, but for now, you can book combined itineraries on China Airlines’ website. For example, you could fly Southwest from Nashville to Ontario and then connect onto a China Airlines Boeing 777-300 for the nonstop trip to Taiwan.\r\n\r\nYou can’t redeem Rapid Rewards for these flights just yet, but once the system’s ready, it’ll make long-haul travel way easier. For now, it’s just a promising start, and we’ll keep you updated with any more news!\r\n\r\n \r\n\r\n**| CRUISES**\r\n\r\nhttps://www.nytimes.com/2025/08/20/t-magazine/small-cruise-ships.html \r\n\r\n**Luxury Cruises for People Who Really Don’t Like Cruises**\r\n\r\nMany know by now my fear and distaste for cruises, but for those like me who might need convincing to at least give it a try, there are small, luxurious ships https://www.nytimes.com/2025/08/20/t-magazine/small-cruise-ships.html you can take that make the first step easier. Think of cozy, well-appointed yachts instead of massive, crowded floating cities with strangers in your face and flash mobs you want to look away from.\r\n\r\nA lot of these new small ships are linked to luxury brands like The Ritz-Carlton, Four Seasons, and Aman, and they’re actually getting people on board who’ve never cruised before. At one travel agency, 78% of Ritz-Carlton guests booked as first-time visitors. You’re also probably sick of me singing its praises. However, when I got an email last week announcing that the annual fee would be jumping from $550 to $795, I gawked at the $245 increase. \r\n\r\nBefore you go canceling the card, though, you should know that the increase comes with some pretty epic new perks as well. But, are they worth it? Let’s take a look.\r\n\r\n&#127881;\r\n\r\n**The new perks**\r\n\r\nThere is a long list of new perks https://thedailynavigator.com/the-chase-sapphire-reserve-just-got-a-major-upgrade/, but I’m only going to highlight a few of my favorites. \r\n\r\n* \r\n\r\n**Earn 8x points on all Chase Travel purchases** (replacing the 5x on flights and 10x on hotels and car rentals).\r\n\r\n* \r\n\r\n**A $500 annual credit towards The Edit,** AKA Chase’s handpicked collection of 1,000+ hotels and resorts plus other perks like a $100 property credit, room upgrade, and more. \r\n\r\n* \r\n\r\n**Platinum Elite status with IHG Hotels & Resorts.** I am \r\n\r\n**really** excited about because I have been on a mission to get more hotel perks. This 1-Day Upgrade Adds Serious Value**\r\n\r\nProtect your home and increase its value in one afternoon.\r\n\r\nHomeBuddy https://www.meintercept.com/3T3CM8/239GQMZ/?uid=15182&sub1=the_navigator&sub2=newsletter&sub3=07_07_2025 connects you with trusted, no-middleman installers who fast-fit durable gutter guards over your current system. You May Be Owed $2,000**\r\n\r\nYou might not believe it, but getting bumped from an overbooked flight is more common than you think https://www.travelandleisure.com/what-to-do-if-your-flight-is-overbooked-11765478, especially during the busy summer season. While the practice of overbooking is annoying at best, the good news is that you’re not entirely powerless when you’ve been bumped. Here’s what to know in case it happens to you and how to avoid getting bumped in the first place.\r\n\r\n✈️\r\n\r\n**What do I do if I get bumped?**\r\n\r\nFirst, check the airline’s app for alternative flights. A lot of the time, you can rebook yourself faster than waiting in line, and then when you get up to the gate agent, you can ask about the reimbursement and proper compensation. Don’t be afraid to ask for things like meal vouchers, a hotel stay if you’re delayed overnight, and even if they can put you on a flight with another airline. \r\n\r\nNow, here’s how you might be owed $2,000. If you’re delayed by one to two hours (or one to four hours on an international flight), you are entitled to 200 percent of your one-way fare, up to $1,075. Delays that last longer than two hours domestically and four hours internationally can earn you up to 400 percent, up to $2,150. Keep in mind that this compensation amount only applies to those who have been involuntarily bumped.\r\n\r\n✅\r\n\r\n**How can I avoid getting bumped?**\r\n\r\nAirlines use a number of factors to pick who to bump and it's certainly not random. Solo travelers are more likely to be bumped because they are easier to rebook. On the other hand, passengers with elite status, people who paid a higher fare, or those who checked in early are less likely to be bumped.\r\n\r\n**With that in mind, you can avoid getting bumped by:**\r\n\r\n* Checking in early\r\n\r\n* Selecting your seat in advance\r\n\r\n* Flying during off-peak hours\r\n\r\n* Flying with an airline you have status or loyalty rewards with. Frequent fliers and elite members (even entry-level status holders) are less likely to be bumped. \r\n\r\n* Book a higher fare class—even within economy. Now, I’m all for saving money, but you might want to weigh the risk. If you know you’re traveling on a busy route during a peak season, you might want to pay a little more for a little more security.\r\n\r\nWant to know more?Read more tips here. https://www.travelandleisure.com/what-to-do-if-your-flight-is-overbooked-11765478\r\n\r\n**| TALES FROM TIME OFF**\r\n\r\nhttps://seatboost.com/ \r\n\r\n**These are just the appetizers.**\r\n\r\n**How I Got Upgraded to Business Class on Condor**\r\n\r\nI just got back from two weeks in Bosnia, and I also flew in business class for the first time, so I thought I’d share my experience and how it all unfolded. \r\n\r\n&#128186;\r\n\r\n**So, how did it happen?**\r\n\r\nInitially, I bought an economy flight with the first leg being LAX to Frankfurt. At the time of booking, I’d hoped to book a business class seat, but when I saw it was a whopping $4,900 for a seat, I knew it wouldn’t be worth my points or cash. So, that’s how I ended up booking economy, which was fine by me. \r\n\r\nThen, I got curious about what Condor’s upgrade policy entailed. Turns out that the airline offers upgrades via an online auction app called SeatBoost https://seatboost.com/. I went on Reddit to read reviews about the app and how much people typically paid. It looked like there were a few things to keep in mind:\r\n\r\n* \r\n\r\n**Don't bother bidding early.** Bidding opens 24 hours before takeoff and closes an hour before departure. Because the highest bid wins, you can wait until the clock almost runs out to see what the highest bids are at that moment and decide if you want to bid more.\r\n\r\n* \r\n\r\n**Only bid the minimum to start.** It turns out there are a lot of bots used on the app. Even when I bid, there was a “John Smith” who bid above me, and I still won because clearly that’s a fake name. (Sorry to any real-life John Smiths out there.)\r\n\r\n* \r\n\r\n**You can win the bid and still might not get the upgrade** if they run out of available seats before the flight takes off.\r\n\r\n* \r\n\r\n**The upgrade is only valid for one-way.** In other words, if you bid on the outgoing flight and win, then you’ll fly out business class on the way over, but on the way back, you’ll be in an economy seat. \r\n\r\n* \r\n\r\n**The pricing is all over the board.** Some people paid $450 for an upgrade, while others paid $900. It depends on a lot of factors, but it’s still significantly cheaper than the original pricing I was seeing.\r\n\r\nLong story short, I bid on the minimum required amount and won!\r\n\r\n&#128064;\r\n\r\n**Was it worth it?**\r\n\r\nAbsolutely. Do I wish I were upgraded on a fancier airline? Sure. (I see you, Emirates!) ButCondor’s seats https://www.fodors.com/news/airlines/review-condor-airlines-new-business-class-cabin and food selection were actually pretty amazing given the low price. They kept bringing out more and more food and snacks to the point where I was worried I wouldn’t get enough time to sleep. I got a few nice perks like toiletries, PJs, and even a tech pouch. \r\n\r\nThe best part, though, was landing completely rested and with no muscle soreness or bloated feet. It was \r\n\r\n**definitely**, without a doubt, worth it for a twelve-hour trip, and I can’t wait to do it again. I probably wouldn’t ever pay full price for a seat, but the cheap upgrade was a win in my book.\r\n\r\n**| TIKTOK OF THE WEEK**\r\n\r\nhttps://www.tiktok.com/@jackiemustian/video/7503238522673827115 \r\n\r\n**Airports are really doing the most…and the least at the same time.**\r\n\r\n**| WHAT ELSE IS HAPPENING**\r\n\r\n* Could travel insurance help cover theseextreme heat waves https://www.euronews.com/travel/2025/07/03/europes-summer-heatwaves-should-you-cancel-your-holiday-when-extreme-weather-hits?\r\n\r\n* 5 things to do if you’re everinjured on vacation https://www.sunset.com/travel/what-to-do-if-injured-on-vacation\r\n\r\n* This airline is the latestcyberattack victim https://www.travelpulse.com/news/airlines-airports/qantas-becomes-latest-victim-of-a-cyberattack\r\n\r\n* I’m happy to see that my hometown was named themost beautiful US city https://www.travelandleisure.com/most-beautiful-city-in-usa-reddit-travelers-11751694\r\n\r\n* Oh no:Sweat stains https://www.usatoday.com/story/travel/news/2025/07/03/tsa-body-scanners-sweat-stains/84446229007/ could cause a travel delay\r\n\r\n* I’m thinking about becoming a ResortPass girl, butis it worth it? We recommend flying budget on short and direct flights for the best results and lowest risk of disaster or having to interact with the sassy RyanAir social media manager on Twitter. \r\n\r\n✈️\r\n\r\n**Book multi-city trips**\r\n\r\nCombine routes to save up to 30 percent and explore more destinations. Multi-city bookings are particularly cost-effective for long-haul international travel. Instead of booking separate one-way tickets, combining them into a multi-city itinerary can save you a considerable amount of money.\r\n\r\n&#128064;\r\n\r\n**Hidden city ticketing**\r\n\r\nSkip the last leg of your flight and use tools like Skiplagged to cut costs by 20-50 percent. Just remember, this sneaky hack does come with some risks. Since your bags are sent to the final destination, this strategy only works if you’re traveling with carry-on luggage. Airlines might also penalize you if they catch on to what you’re doing. So do this hack sparingly and wisely. \r\n\r\n&#128270;\r\n\r\n**Take advantage of flight search tools**\r\n\r\nPlatforms like Google Flights, Kayak, andDollar Flight Club https://dollarflightclub.com/ help you compare fares and set alerts for deals. Try to set your alerts well in advance. Keep in mind that the best time to book domestic flights is about 38 days out, while international flights are about 101 days out. \r\n\r\nWe still have five more tips for you! https://dollarflightclub.com/articles/10-hacks-for-finding-cheap-flights-and-transfers/ \r\n\r\nRead up on all of them here https://dollarflightclub.com/articles/10-hacks-for-finding-cheap-flights-and-transfers/ and get ready to save.\r\n\r\nhttps://www.threads.com/@themuffreport/post/DKe_vuiswJB \r\n\r\n**I am convinced the people at the State Department do this kind of stuff on purpose.**\r\n\r\nHaving trouble keeping up with all the travel news? Many in the US, Brazil, Spain, and Greece are sticking to domestic travel, while people in the Netherlands and Belgium are exploring nearby cities.\r\n\r\n**Three major summer trends:**\r\n\r\n**&#127836; Foodie cities that beat the crowds:** Travelers are ditching major foodie destinations for more affordable, underrated spots. Instead of Paris, they’re heading to Rotterdam. Instead of Positano, it’s Alassio on Italy’s northern coast. Some of the hottest up-and-coming food spots this summer include Chiclana de la Frontera in Spain, Belo Horizonte in Brazil, Shillong in India, and Rawai in Thailand.\r\n\r\n**&#127908; Fandom travel is on the rise:** People are planning trips around their favorite artists and sports teams, especially Gen Z women. Concerts are the second biggest reason people are vacationing this summer (after family visits). Cities like Houston and Manchester are seeing massive search spikes thanks to big-name tours and matches. Whether it’s Beyoncé in Paris (I’ll see you there) or a rugby game in Perth, fandom-fueled travel is taking over.\r\n\r\n**&#129523; Spontaneous staycations are in:** Americans are staying closer to home this summer, likely due to the state of the economy and the dollar not going far in Europe. Most trips are happening within 300 miles, with guests opting for last-minute getaways to cabins, beaches, and quiet towns. There’s so much to know, so sit back and save this for later! \r\n\r\n**✈️ Find flights through a chat box**\r\n\r\nI love that Gemini lets you just type what you want and spits out flight options. It pulled info from a past conference and suggested flights, hotels, and even a restaurant that matched my past choices.\r\n\r\n**&#128373;️‍♀️ Use AI to fact-check AI**\r\n\r\nEven the smartest AI can mess up sometimes and not be fully accurate. I had Gemini list restaurants, hotels, and flights, then double-checked everything. Most of it was solid, but a couple of details were technically right but a little off for the vibe I wanted. AI is great, but human eyes and research still matter.\r\n\r\nHopefully the next time you open up the search engine to browse a trip, you remember these tricks to make it all easier on you.\r\n\r\n \r\n\r\n**| CRUISES**\r\n\r\nhttps://www.cntraveler.com/story/new-africa-cruise-itineraries-are-coming-to-both-sides-of-the-continent \r\n\r\n**Explore Africa by Sea on New Itineraries**\r\n\r\nAfrica hasn’t exactly been a hotspot for cruises, until now. They seem to be all the rage in travel trends and I’m excited about it. New itineraries https://www.cntraveler.com/story/new-africa-cruise-itineraries-are-coming-to-both-sides-of-the-continent are popping up on both sides of the continent, giving travelers a chance to see more without the usual crowds that you bump into on land. \r\n\r\n**Itineraries around the continent: **\r\n\r\n&#127480;&#127464; Seychelles & Outer Islands - Smaller ships like Ponant’s Le Bougainville https://us.ponant.com/cruises/on-board/vessel-le-bougainville can access remote spots with minimal impact. You can look forward to “wet landings” onto tiny islands like St. Francois and a bunch of wildlife (more than people).\r\n\r\n&#127481;&#127487; Tanzania & Seychelles -  Aqua Lares https://www.aquaexpeditions.com/cruise-ships/aqua-lareslaunches in February and will combine cruising along the coast with eco-friendly island hopping.\r\n\r\n&#127487;&#127462;Cape Town to Lisbon - Oceania Cruises’ 24-day African Revelation https://www.oceaniacruises.com/cruises/SIR260331/ sails the Atlantic coast with stops to Namibia’s sand dunes, Abidjan, Monkey Hill in Ghana, and Dakar, Senegal.\r\n\r\n&#127487;&#127462;South Africa & Namibia by Sea - Smithsonian and Ponant explore the Cape of Good Hope and private game reserves like Addo Elephant National Park and Sibuya.\r\n\r\nIf you're eager to see parts of Africa that are typically hard to reach, these cruises offer that unique access that’s hard to pass up on especially if you have the means to splurge on the trips. Costs for each cruise vary but start at around $6,500 per person.
